# All vocal and live mic-path multi-effects — comparison

[Guitar](ALL-GUITAR.md) · [Bass](ALL-BASS.md) · [Vocal](ALL-VOCAL.md) · [Category](MULTI-EFFECTS.md) · [Repository](README.md)

All 56 recorded vocal or live mic-path multi-effects: 22 vocal-only and 34 with independent acoustic/general-instrument processing. Guitar-thru/chord detection is not a processed guitar path. FV01 remains excluded in [SINGLE-VOCAL.md](SINGLE-VOCAL.md); a mic input does not imply harmony or pitch correction.

These are **comparisons of the recorded catalogue**, not exhaustive market coverage or fresh stock certification. Organized 2026-10-16 from the linked source records; their original technical/price dates, firmware, revision and access limits still apply. Every model remains partial. **? means unverified, not No.** Read the complete linked record for sources and conflicting evidence; this view does not silently resolve those conflicts.

K/F/B = independently adjustable rotary controls / footswitches / separate buttons; toe and power switches are additional unless explicitly stated. Body axes and mass qualifications are retained. Color does not prove touch; USB audio does not prove bus power; IRs, native NAM, converted NAM and proprietary captures differ.

MS,M4 | **Editor platforms:** **Pro Control full editor: Windows10 x64 22H2/Windows11,macOS12.7+,iOS13.1+,Android8 arm64-v8a+.** Mobile editing is documented, not merely Bluetooth audio. Do not label the older English headline the latest known firmware. The newer version also renames FBE/Freeman models to Forge; old names in an earlier guide are not additional models. R,J<br>**Looper / live:** **Looper expanded from the product page's60 seconds to three minutes in1.7.53.** Three polysynths and MIDI-over-USB support were added in that same release. The June2026 update adds8 amps,8 cabinets and15 effects; these are update increments, not the complete library and not to be added again to an updated model list. Scene/snapshot functions, songs/setlists,tuner and delay/reverb spillover are documented; no measured global latency is asserted. R,J,P |

[Core product](https://www.headrushfx.com/products/core/index.html), [Core FAQ](https://support.headrushfx.com/en/support/solutions/articles/69000844379-headrush-core-frequently-asked-questions).<br>**Core connections:** The published port bullets incorrectly call SEND an input and RETURN an output. Functional directions are corrected here rather than copying the reversal. Obtain the matching Core manual/rear-label confirmation before treating the port/power list as fully reconciled. The IEC/DC conflict is not silently resolved by borrowing another HeadRush model. | **Power:** **12 V DC,3 A,center-positive adapter input** in technical specifications; FAQ instead says IEC, unresolved documentation conflict. Battery/USB-powered operation **not established** — Evidence: [Core product](https://www.headrushfx.com/products/core/index.html), [Core FAQ](https://support.headrushfx.com/en/support/solutions/articles/69000844379-headrush-core-frequently-asked-questions) | **Editor:** Wi-Fi **browser editor Windows/macOS/iOS/Android**; Linux not established. Windows/macOS firmware updaters and USB file transfer are separate functions — Evidence: [Release notes,p.7](https://cdn.inmusicbrands.com/Software/SGEE/52/READ%20ME%20-%20HeadRush%20Core%20Firmware%20Update%20Instructions%20v5.2.0.pdf)<br>**USB audio:** **6-in/4-out**,24-bit44.1/48/96 kHz; recording/reamping; Windows driver,macOS driverless — Evidence: [Core FAQ](https://support.headrushfx.com/en/support/solutions/articles/69000844379-headrush-core-frequently-asked-questions) | **Live features:** Scenes,trails; looper **50 layers,5 minutes/layer,20 minutes total per loop**; drum machine — Evidence: [Core FAQ](https://support.headrushfx.com/en/support/solutions/articles/69000844379-headrush-core-frequently-asked-questions), [Core product](https://www.headrushfx.com/products/core/index.html)<br>**Mic routing / live:** [Core FAQ](https://support.headrushfx.com/en/support/solutions/articles/69000844379-headrush-core-frequently-asked-questions),rechecked2026-10-17: guitar-centric Straight/Middle Split/Immediate Split paths do not permit the normal vocal-FX chain. **Mic Dry Out** instead has gate,compression,high-pass and simpleEQ adjustments; retaining14 guitar blocks is not14 additional vocal blocks.<br>**Core firmware and category-count evidence:** **Firmware5.2.0,October2026** appears on printedp.1 of the official release notes, visually checked in the original research. [Original manual](https://media.rockboard.de/Artikell_PDF/Manual_PDF/Mooer/EN/ME_GS1000_Manual_EN.pdf) and [regional product](https://mooer.jp/products/mooer-gs1000). | **Power / battery / USB:** **DC 9V 2A center-negative**, supplied adapter. Standard version has no integrated operating battery; Li's 9V 3A adapter, 7.4V 4750mAh battery and conditional six-hour rating must not be copied here. USB-bus operation unknown<br>**Supply:** DC input; front power pushbutton; rear 48Vtoggle. No speaker-power output | **Desktop editor:** **MOOER Studio Windows 10+ and macOS 10.13+** in current exact-model download page; original manual says macOS 10.11+, retained as dated conflict. Do not equate cloud exchange with a full mobile parameter editor<br>**Drivers / USB audio:** Separate Windows ASIO driver; original Mac audio plug-and-play. **2in/2out, 24bit44.1–192kHz USB**, wet/dry channel selection, hardware/software monitor mix and reamp.192kHz USB support is separate from44.1kHz internal ADC and IR specs | **Routing architecture:** Five templates: Serial, Dual, Split, Mix, Parallel. Independent input/output nodes, movable split/mix; dual chains can separately process instrument and microphone. Mix cannot precede split or share its position<br>**Scene / sub-patch control:** **3 SubPatches per preset** with module states and parameter mappings, plus stomp/tap/single/multiple/A–B CTRL functions. F4 wireless/MIDI can extend control<br>**Looper / drums:** **480 seconds/8minutes**, overdub, undo/redo, auto-record threshold, independent levels, drum sync, count-in and loop import/export. Global product prose56 drum kits conflicts with its54kits+4 metronome bullet; exact current rhythm inventory unresolved<br>**FX loop:** One 6.35mm stereo TRS SEND andone RETURN, unbalanced stereo; TRS-to-dualTS breakout for stereo external effects. ## Scope and provenance

Instrument views overlap and must not be summed. All 287 active models count once in `research-state.json`; the six explicitly excluded archives never enter these tables. Former/donated eligible devices remain without private receipt/order/payment details. Root brand records take precedence where consolidated; legacy sources remain for full evidence until a checked lossless merge. No GitHub workflow is used.
